| Z2-N-BUDGET-434 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1480 | Zenith2 Budgets › Production-quality acceptance › CinematicSlice acceptance | CinematicSlice's acceptance package exercises character and animation quality, cinematics, lighting and image quality, audio, and set-pieces, including cinematic start, interrupt and skip with gameplay-state restore. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-435 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1482 | Zenith2 Budgets › Production-quality acceptance › CinematicSlice acceptance | CinematicSlice's sign-off list includes at least: facial animation and lip-sync; character skin, hair-card and cloth appearance within the classified simulations; cinematic camera framing and depth of field; the audio mix and music transitions; set-piece readability; and the absence of visible pops on cinematic skip. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-436 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1486 | Zenith2 Budgets › Production-quality acceptance › Game2D acceptance | Game2D's acceptance package exercises 2D, UI, accessibility flows and localization, including the whole menus, settings and first 10 minutes completed with a screen reader, with remapped controls, with reduced motion and with a colour-blind mode, each a scripted acceptance run. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-437 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1488 | Zenith2 Budgets › Production-quality acceptance › Game2D acceptance | Game2D's sign-off list includes at least: pixel-perfect sprite presentation at every supported output scale; UI navigation and focus clarity with a controller and with touch; screen-reader narration usefulness; colour-blind mode legibility; and pseudo-localized and right-to-left layouts. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-438 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1492 | Zenith2 Budgets › Production-quality acceptance › Animation budgeting and PSO preparation | Animation budgeting (significance-based update-rate reduction) and PSO preparation (precaching from recorded and predicted PSO sets, the missing-PSO fallback, and no first-use stalls) are implementation tickets in ANIM and RPROD respectively, not only performance targets; their rows are the animation phase rows and the missing-PSO and cold-cache rows of this document. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-439 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1496 | Zenith2 Budgets › Production-quality acceptance › Performance acceptance evidence | The performance part of every stage acceptance is: every workload that exists at that stage measured per this document on the reference desktop (both backends) and the S24, with evidence records whose fingerprints match the accepted tree, meeting every adopted row they measure. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-440 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1497 | Zenith2 Budgets › Production-quality acceptance › Performance acceptance evidence | The Stage 6 acceptance additionally includes the sustained thermal runs of WS-SUSTAIN, the package and install-size rows, and the physical confirmation of MP-LATENCY. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-441 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1498 | Zenith2 Budgets › Production-quality acceptance › Performance acceptance evidence | Every stage sprint's mid-sprint integration check measures the flagship slices' playthrough scripts (or, before the slices exist, the workloads that do) on the reference desktop and the S24 against these rows and files every breach as a BUG ticket, so drift is caught before the stage acceptance. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-461 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1504 | Zenith2 Budgets › Row index | The Row index lists every row of this document exactly once, with the same ID, name, status, value, unit and producer as the row's own table, and is regenerated in the same diff as any row change. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-462 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1895 | Zenith2 Budgets › Row index › Producer tickets | Every distinct producer named by a pending row is listed below with the rows it allocates, and the `needs-human` ruling ticket that allocates those rows carries exactly this producer name as its title prefix; its harness ticket, where "Producer ticket shape" gives it one, carries the same name with `ticket` replaced by `harness`. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-463 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:75 | Zenith2 Budgets › Purpose versioning and change rule › Producer ticket shape | The Stage 1 per-system budget ticket for ASSET is the `needs-human` ruling that allocates the rows the table "Producer tickets" lists for it; its harness half, the "Stage 1 per-system budget harness for ASSET", is the ticket that delivers `Zenith_Bench_AssetLoading` under `Zenith2/Benchmarks/AssetLoading/` (the ASSET benchmark, not a harness under `Zenith2/Benchmarks/Budgets/ASSET/`) with its `--smoke` CTest run, per `Zenith2/Docs/Design/ASSET.md` → "Stage 1". |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-464 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:914 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Engine service CPU rows | The row "Scene activation slice per frame prologue" is the MP-CPUZONE span of the `Scene.ActivateSlice` zone (frame prologue step 7, main thread: the sliced out-of-tick activation of a prepared scene into an isolated world before a world-transition swap, `Zenith2/Docs/Design/SCENE/Loading.md`), measured on Windows and the S24 in the workloads that perform world transitions; the configuration key `scene.activationBudgetUs` takes the row's value in microseconds as its engine-default layer, and a slice that reaches it stops at the next chunk boundary and continues in the next prologue (BR-DEFER, counted by the deferral counter); it is a member of `CPU-SIM`, allocated by the Stage 1 per-system budget ticket for SCENE beside Z2-B-SCENE-001 and Z2-B-SCENE-002. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-465 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:986 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Latency and duration rows | The SAVE rows "Save write duration per save" (counter `save.write.latency_us`), "Save load duration per save" (`save.load.last_us`), "World restore duration per 10,000 entities" (`save.restore.us_per_10k_entities`) and "Save file size per slot" (`save.write.last_bytes`, in "Package and storage rows") are MP-COUNTER rows whose statistic is the maximum over the run, allocated by the Stage 1 per-system budget ticket for SAVE (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/SAVE.md` → "Pending row needs"); the replay log has no row, being bounded by the configuration limit `replay.maxLogBytes`. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-466 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:987 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Latency and duration rows | The row "Audio output latency" is the p95 over the run of `audio.device.latency_us`, read once per 100 ms after a 10 s warm-up, on Windows with a wired output and on the S24 speaker; the row "Audio stream underruns per run" is the total of `audio.streams.underruns` over the run, 0 tolerated. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-467 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:988 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Latency and duration rows | The row "Animation stream stalls per run" is the total of `anim.stream.stalls_delta` over a W-Cinematic run, 0 tolerated in the flagship. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-468 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:863 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Frame-phase CPU rows | The rows "Animation budgeting reduced-rate CPU", "Animation full-rate evaluations per tick cap", "Animation stream cache bytes", "Animation stream stalls per run" and "Clip compression error" are allocated by the ANIM Stage 4 budget ruling ticket (`needs-human`, ANIM's epic, fix version `Z2 Stage 4`, blocked by ANIM's update-rate budgeting, clip streaming and clip compression tickets), because their mechanisms land at Stage 4; the reduced-rate row is phase 3's MP-CPUZONE span with significance reduction enabled, reported beside Z2-B-ANIM-002 (measured unreduced) and excluded from every bound; the per-platform values of the evaluations cap and the stream cache are the values of `animation.budget.maxFullRateEvaluationsPerTick` and `animation.streaming.cacheBytes`; the stream cache is a member of Z2-B-ANIM-001; "Animation extraction CPU" is the span of `Animation.ExtractPoses`, inside the span of Z2-B-RPROD-003, so it is reported and excluded from every bound; the Stage 1 per-system budget ticket for ANIM allocates it beside Z2-B-ANIM-001 … -003. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-469 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:910 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Engine service CPU rows | The AUDIO rows "Audio stream decode CPU" (MP-COUNTER `audio.decode.us_delta`, p95 over frames), "Streamed voice count" (`audio.streams.active`), "Audio instance capacity per world" (`audio.instances.live`), "Audio command queue capacity per frame" (`audio.commands.high_water`) and "Audio occlusion tests per frame" (`audio.occlusion.rays_delta`), each a maximum over the run unless stated, are allocated by the Stage 1 per-system budget ticket for AUDIO, with the counters, statistics and breach consequences of `Zenith2/Docs/Design/AUDIO.md` → "Pending new rows". |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-470 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:913 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Engine service CPU rows | The row "VFX lifecycle CPU" is the MP-CPUZONE span of the zone `VFX.Lifecycle` and is a member of `CPU-SIM`; the row "VFX trigger queue capacity per world" is the per-world trigger queue of ZenithVFX, whose counter `vfx.triggers.dropped` stays 0 for every script-accepted trigger, and its value is a script reserve of Z2-B-SCRIPT-024 × Z2-B-JOBS-002 plus the C++ share the Stage 1 per-system budget ticket for VFX rules (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/VFX.md` → "Pending row needs"). |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-471 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1263 | Zenith2 Budgets › Scripting rows › Scripting row table | The value of Z2-B-SCRIPT-025 "Save-request capacity per tick" is SAVE's `save.maxRequestsPerTick`, one number: script requests above it are merge rejections (BR-MERGE) and C++ requests above it return `SAVE_ERROR_REQUEST_LIMIT`, and SAVE declares no second limit. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-472 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:572 | Zenith2 Budgets › Memory budgets › Per-subsystem memory rows | The Stage 1 per-system budget ticket for CORE allocates Z2-B-CORE-005 "Frame and graph arenas" with, as an input, the render-world slot-arena demand each render-world writer states ([Z2-N-REND-017]), VFX's per-world maxima being `Zenith2/Docs/Design/VFX/Simulation.md` → "CPU particle output"; the later VFX ticket confirms that demand and does not rewrite the CORE row. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-473 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:959 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Shared capacity rows | The row "Cell activation entities per tick" is the value of `stream.activation.maxEntitiesPerTick`, below Z2-B-ECS-003: a cell activation that would exceed it in a tick continues in the next tick (BR-DEFER); the cook's per-cell entity limit is min(16,384, the configured `ecs.maxSpawnsPerTick`), so the row stays below Z2-B-ECS-003 (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/STREAM.md` → "Budget rows"). |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-474 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:960 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Shared capacity rows | The four PHYS capacity rows are the values of `physics.maxBodies`, `physics.maxConstraints`, `physics.maxContactRecordsPerTick` and `physics.maxActiveRagdolls` (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/PHYS/ComponentsAPI.md`): a body or constraint creation beyond its capacity fails with `PHYSICS_ERROR_BODY_CAPACITY` or `PHYSICS_ERROR_CONSTRAINT_CAPACITY` (BR-REFUSE), a contact record beyond the per-tick capacity drops the record with the greatest sort key, counted in `physics.contacts.dropped_delta` (BR-DROP), and a ragdoll enter beyond the cap is refused (BR-REFUSE); each is allocated by the PHYS per-system ticket. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-475 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:861 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Frame-phase CPU rows | The row "Physics step CPU" (Z2-B-PHYS-002) is the sum, per tick, of the MP-CPUZONE spans of `Physics.Step`, `Physics.SyncTransforms`, `Physics.RagdollBlend` and `Physics.DeferredQueries`, excluding `Physics.CharacterMove`, which Z2-B-GAME-001 covers; its value is the selected backend's per platform; its W-Game2D column measures those four nodes when the game selects the `Physics` module, and is dropped from the row in D-SEL-2DPHYS's (open, Stage 2) ruling diff if that ruling places 2D physics in TWOD's library. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-476 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1300 | Zenith2 Budgets › Editor iteration rows › Iteration targets | The editor rows of `Zenith2/Docs/Design/EDIT.md` → "Pending row needs" are allocated by the EDIT per-system ticket: "Editor frame CPU" is the median frame's MP-CPUZONE span of `Editor.Frame` with W-Editor's scene open; "Editor projection apply CPU per 1,000 changes" is the MP-EDIT time of applying 1,000 projected changes; "Editor undo history memory" is the MP-SUBMEM size of the undo history, editor only and outside the Player totals ([Z2-N-BUDGET-143]), and its value sets `editor.undo.maxBytes`; "Animation graph preview latency" (ANIM's row) is the MP-EDIT time from a graph edit to the first frame drawing the recompiled pose. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-477 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:911 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Engine service CPU rows | The row "Sprite batching and 2D preparation CPU" (Z2-B-TWOD-001) is the MP-CPUZONE sum, per frame, of the spans of `TwoD.Extract`, `Flux.Sprite2D.Prepare` and `Flux.Sprite2D.Write`; the row "2D physics step CPU" is the per-tick sum of the spans of `TwoD.Physics2DStep` and `TwoD.Physics2DSyncTransforms`, and the row "2D animation CPU" the per-tick sum of `TwoD.Animate` and `TwoD.AnimateSkeletons` (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/TWOD.md` → "Pending row needs"). |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-478 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:912 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Engine service CPU rows | The row "2D sprite passes GPU" times the passes `Sprite2DScatter`, `Sprite2DCanvas` and `Sprite2DWorld`, and the row "2D canvas composite GPU" the pass `Sprite2DComposite`; the row "2D physics world memory" is the MP-SUBMEM of the selected 2D physics backend's resident bytes, "TWOD module resident memory" that of the module's record tables, poses, overlays and memory consumer, and "2D renderer memory" the MP-SUBMEM of the RENDERING subsystem reported through `Flux_Sprite2DStats::m_ulResidentBytes` (the CPU chunk mirror, the GPU chunk pool and the tile table); D-SEL-2DPHYS (open, Stage 2) compares against the 2D physics step and world memory rows (`Zenith2/Docs/DecisionLog.md` → "D-SEL-2DPHYS"); each W-Game2D cell is measured at the content scale the Stage 1 flagship acceptance ticket for Game2D commits, each W-UI2D cell at the scale of UI's Stage 1 workload scale ticket for W-UI2D. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-479 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:961 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Shared capacity rows | The six AI capacity rows are per world and are the values of AI's configuration keys (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/AI.md` → "Pending row needs"): "Navigation tile rebuilds per tick" is `ai.nav.maxTileRebuildsPerTick`, "Path node expansions per tick" the per-world search expansion budget (64 searches × 512 expansions by default), "Sight rays per tick" `ai.perception.maxRaysPerTick` and "Noise stimuli per tick" `ai.perception.maxNoisesPerTick`; work beyond the first three continues in a later tick (BR-DEFER) and a noise beyond the fourth is dropped (BR-DROP); "Agent capacity per world" is `ai.agent.maxAgents`, and an agent creation beyond it is refused (BR-REFUSE); "Navigation loads in flight" is `ai.nav.maxLoadsInFlight`, the load records counted by `ai.nav.loads_in_flight` from completion-ID allocation to publication, and a load beyond it waits for a free record (BR-DEFER). Each is allocated by the AI per-system ticket. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-480 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:962 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Shared capacity rows | The row "Asynchronous path requests in flight" (Z2-B-AI-003) is the path service's active set, counted by `ai.path.active`: a request beyond it is deferred (BR-DEFER, counted by `ai.path.deferred_delta`), whether a C++ caller or a script made it; a script's path request is first capped at the merge by Z2-B-SCRIPT-026's pathfinding value (ruling Q19 (a)), and an accepted request then enters the active set like any other. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-481 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:860 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Frame-phase CPU rows | The row "AI and navigation phase CPU" (Z2-B-AI-002) is the p95 MP-CPUZONE span of the whole of frame phase 7, which also contains GAME's `Gameplay.Rules` and `Gameplay.Cameras`; GAME's pending sub-row "Gameplay framework CPU" is reported inside it (BR-REPORT) and is never summed into `CPU-SIM` a second time (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/GAME.md` → "Rows measured against"). |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-482 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:74 | Zenith2 Budgets › Purpose versioning and change rule › Producer ticket shape | SHIP's per-system ruling, the "Stage 3 per-system budget ticket for SHIP", allocates Z2-B-SHIP-015 to -018 at Stage 3, not Stage 1: its Z2-D-084 harness half is DEVT's `Zenith2/Benchmarks/Run-Workload.ps1` (drafted in the Stage 3 sprint), and the WS-FRAME workloads it measures have no measurable content before Stage 3; it is `blocks`-linked from DEVT's `Run-Workload.ps1` tickets and from SHIP's own `RowSources.json` entries ticket as well as from the CORE, JOBS and RPROD rulings (ruling X365-SHIP-BUDGET-S3, 2026-10-16). |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-483 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1328 | Zenith2 Budgets › Gate budget rows | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets/GateRows.md` is part of this document: every rule here that names this document, its rows, its row tables or its sections applies to that file's rows, tables and sections exactly as to this file's, and its rows appear in "Row index" like every other row. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-484 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:657 | Zenith2 Budgets › Package and storage budgets › Package and storage rows | The PACK rows of `Zenith2/Docs/Design/PACK.md` → "Pending new rows": the three Play rows (asset pack download size, total download size and pack count) are download sizes that exist only after Gradle and bundletool, so they are BR-ACCEPT, and `<Game>_Bundle` additionally refuses an adopted value as a build failure (`Invoke-Zenith2Package.ps1 -Bundle`, exit code 8, naming the row); "Package service resident memory" is the MP-SUBMEM size of the installer's records and verification arena, the delivery cells, the event queue, the world records, the delivery-state stamp table and the verified stamp file copy, fixed at `Create` (no governor consumer, so BR-ACCEPT), and a member of `MEM-WIN-RAM` and so of `MEM-S24`; "Asset pack verification throughput floor" is MP-PKGVERIFY; "Cold-boot update verification time" is W-Load's MP-BOOT run on an install carrying a committed 1 GiB content patch and an installed 1 GiB Windows DLC (Win), or a delivered 1 GiB fast-follow pack (S24), minus the same run without them, divided by the GiB present, median of 10 repetitions, measured at the first start after the install (full hash) and at the second (stamp checks only, `Zenith2/Docs/Design/PACK/Algorithms.md` → "Verified cache"), each reported separately; W-Load's cold-boot rows Z2-B-SHIP-013 and Z2-B-SHIP-014 are measured with no update installed, and this row bounds the increment an installed update adds and is never removed. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-485 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1302 | Zenith2 Budgets › Editor iteration rows › Iteration targets | The CONTENT editor rows of `Zenith2/Docs/Design/CONTENT.md` → "Pending new rows": "Batch reference repair time per 1,000 documents" is the MP-EDIT time of a move of 1,000 assets referenced from 1,000 scene documents of the W-Editor workload, each scene referencing 10 of the assets spread across the folder, from `Plan` to COMPLETED (planning, chunk packing and the per-chunk repetition of repairs included), and the measurement reports the plan's document-repair count beside the time; "Semantic merge time of a 16 MiB scene" is the MP-EDIT time of `ZenithContent merge` over the W-Editor workload's largest scene with 1% of its entities changed on each side; "Shared DDC hit ratio on a warm incremental cook" is the cook report's `ddcSharedHits` over `items` on a second host after one host cooked the slice, a reported row (BR-REPORT). |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-486 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1303 | Zenith2 Budgets › Editor iteration rows › Iteration targets | The WORLD rows of `Zenith2/Docs/Design/WORLD.md` → "Pending row needs" are tool-side rows of the W-Editor workload on OpenWorldSlice's world: "World partition import time" is the MP-EDIT time of the `world.world` import, clean and after a one-region edit (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/WORLD/Partition.md` → "Partition cost and scaling"); "Lighting bake time per cell" and "Edit-world region open latency" are MP-EDIT times; "World partition import memory" and "Lighting bake tracer memory" are the MP-SUBMEM peaks of tool processes, outside every Player bound. |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-487 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:1304 | Zenith2 Budgets › Editor iteration rows › Iteration targets | The row "Lip-sync import time per audio minute" is the MP-EDIT time of `char.lipsync` imports divided by the imported audio minutes, and the row "Modular part extraction overhead" is the MP-CPUZONE cost of `Animation.ExtractPoses` added by the W-Characters modular variant's part entities, reported inside the span of Z2-B-RPROD-003 and excluded from every bound (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/CHAR.md` → "Pending row needs"). |
| Z2-N-BUDGET-488 | `Zenith2/Docs/Budgets.md`:963 | Zenith2 Budgets › Per-system rows › Shared capacity rows | The row "Video decode CPU" is the MP-CPUZONE span of the `Zenith.VideoDec` thread's decode zone per decoded second of 1080p30 video, on a dedicated thread outside the frame graph's simulation and render paths, so it is a member of no CPU bound; the row "Cinematic instances per world" is the value of `cine.maxInstancesPerWorld`, and a start beyond it is refused (BR-REFUSE) (`Zenith2/Docs/Design/CINE.md` → "Pending row needs"). |
